  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Berasal dari bahasa Mapudungun kawel (lumba-lumba) dan che (orang), makhluk ini hadir sebagai entitas air magis dalam mitologi Chiloé [cahuelche-c01]. Makhluk ini berpenampilan menyerupai atau identik dengan lumba-lumba tonina (Cephalorhynchus eutropia) sehingga sukar dibedakan secara kasat mata [cahuelche-c02].",
      "en": "Derived from the indigenous roots for dolphin and person, this magical aquatic being belongs to Chilota lore [cahuelche-c01], appearing identical to native dolphins (Cephalorhynchus eutropia) [cahuelche-c02].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cahuelche-c01",
        "cahuelche-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Makhluk ini konon dianugerahi kecerdasan tinggi serta suara pekikan ajaib untuk berkomunikasi kembali dengan umat manusia di laut [cahuelche-c03].",
      "en": "Endowed with supernatural intellect, it utters magical calls to bridge communication with humankind [cahuelche-c03].",

      "source_id": "mama-d-leau-s1",
      "quote": "Mama D'Leau (derived from the French Maman de l'eau or \"Mother of the River\"), also known as Mama Dlo and Mama Glo",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mama D'Leau berarti Ibu Sungai dan dikenal juga sebagai Mama Dlo atau Mama Glo.",
        "en": "Mama D'Leau derives from French meaning Mother of the River, known also as Mama Dlo and Mama Glo."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-d-leau-c02",
      "source_id": "mama-d-leau-s1",
      "quote": "is the protector and healer of all river animals, according to the folklore of islands such as Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago and Dominica.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia adalah pelindung dan penyembuh satwa sungai di Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago, dan Dominica.",
        "en": "She protects and heals river animals in Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago, and Dominica."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mama-d-leau-c03",
      "source_id": "mama-d-leau-s1",
      "quote": "She is usually depicted as a beautiful woman with long hair, who sits on upper body and arms and from her waist downwards twists into coils.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Digambarkan sebagai wanita jelita berambut panjang dengan tubuh bagian bawah melingkar seperti ular.",
        "en": "Depicted as a beautiful woman whose body twists into coils below the waist."
      }
    }
  ],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "topic": "Sisir emas pemikat manusia",
      "reason": "Mitos sisir emas yang ditinggalkannya di atas batu sungai belum diulas secara lengkap."
    }
  ]
}

    "en": "The Mono Grande (\"Large Monkey\") is a large monkey-like creature occasionally reported in South America [mono-grande-c01].",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mono Grande adalah makhluk besar mirip monyet yang sesekali dilaporkan di Amerika Selatan [mono-grande-c01]. Makhluk ini dilaporkan jauh lebih besar daripada monyet Dunia Baru yang dikenal [mono-grande-c02], tetapi laporannya kurang dikenal dan jarang menarik minat para ahli [mono-grande-c03].",
      "en": "The Mono Grande is a large monkey-like creature occasionally reported in South America [mono-grande-c01]. It is said to be much larger than the known New World monkeys [mono-grande-c02], but the reports received little publicity and little interest from experts [mono-grande-c03].",

Foto \"kera De Loys\" tahun 1920 kemudian diidentifikasi hampir pasti sebagai monyet laba-laba [mono-grande-c05], dan Rolf Blomberg menduga desas-desus monster Amazon terilhami buku The Lost World karya Arthur Conan Doyle [mono-grande-c07].",
      "en": "In the early 19th century, Alexander von Humboldt heard stories from the Orinoco about furry human-like creatures called Salvaje [mono-grande-c04], which he considered myths brought by European colonists [mono-grande-c06]. The 1920 photograph of \"De Loys's ape\" was later identified as almost certainly a spider monkey [mono-grande-c05], and Rolf Blomberg speculated that rumours of Amazon monsters were inspired by Arthur Conan Doyle's The Lost World [mono-grande-c07].",

      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "The Mono Grande (Spanish for \"Large Monkey\"), a large monkey-like creature, has been occasionally reported in South America.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mono Grande adalah makhluk besar mirip monyet yang sesekali dilaporkan terlihat di Amerika Selatan.",
        "en": "The Mono Grande is a large monkey-like creature occasionally reported in South America."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c02",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "Such creatures are reported as being much larger than the commonly accepted New World monkeys.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini dilaporkan jauh lebih besar daripada monyet Dunia Baru yang dikenal.",
        "en": "Such creatures are reported as much larger than the known New World monkeys."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c03",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "These accounts have received rather little publicity, and typically generated little or no interest from experts.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan-laporan ini kurang dikenal dan umumnya tidak menarik minat para ahli.",
        "en": "These accounts received little publicity and generally little or no interest from experts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c04",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "The German naturalist Alexander von Humboldt, who travelled in South America during early 19th century, heard stories from Orinoco about furry human-like creatures called Salvaje (\"Wild\"), which were rumoured to capture women, build huts and to occasionally eat human flesh.",
      "locator": "Older reports and sightings",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada awal abad ke-19, naturalis Jerman Alexander von Humboldt mendengar cerita dari Orinoco tentang makhluk berbulu mirip manusia bernama Salvaje yang konon menculik perempuan, membangun gubuk, dan kadang memakan daging manusia.",
        "en": "In the early 19th century, the German naturalist Alexander von Humboldt heard stories from the Orinoco about furry human-like creatures called Salvaje, rumoured to capture women, build huts and occasionally eat human flesh."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c05",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "The so-called De Loys's ape was photographed by Swiss geologist Francois de Loys in 1920 and proposed as a possible unknown great ape of South America; it has since been identified as almost certainly a spider monkey",
      "locator": "Modern reports and sightings",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kera De Loys, yang dipotret ahli geologi Swiss Francois de Loys pada tahun 1920 dan diajukan sebagai kera besar yang belum dikenal, kemudian diidentifikasi hampir pasti sebagai monyet laba-laba.",
        "en": "De Loys's ape, photographed by the Swiss geologist Francois de Loys in 1920 and proposed as an unknown great ape, was later identified as almost certainly a spider monkey."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c06",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "Humboldt considered the reports of Salvaje to be just myths that came to South America with European colonists.",
      "locator": "Criticism",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Humboldt menganggap laporan tentang Salvaje hanyalah mitos yang dibawa ke Amerika Selatan oleh kolonis Eropa.",
        "en": "Humboldt considered the Salvaje reports to be myths brought to South America by European colonists."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mono-grande-c07",
      "source_id": "mono-grande-s1",
      "quote": "The Swedish author Rolf Blomberg speculates (1966) that rumours of hidden monsters in the Amazon basin might have been inspired by Arthur Conan Doyle's book The Lost World (1912)",

      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",

    "en": "Papa Bois is a folklore character of St. Lucia, Dominica, Grenada and Trinidad and Tobago, known as the \"keeper of the forest\" and protector of its flora and fauna [papa-bois-c01, papa-bois-c02].",

    {
      "id": "Papa Bois, dari kata patois Prancis yang berarti \"bapak hutan\", adalah tokoh cerita rakyat St. Lucia, Dominica, Grenada, serta Trinidad and Tobago [papa-bois-c01]. Ia disebut \"penjaga hutan\" dan dianggap pelindung flora dan faunanya [papa-bois-c02], dan ia menikah dengan Mama Dlo [papa-bois-c03].",
      "en": "Papa Bois, from a French patois word meaning \"father of the forest\", is a folklore character of St. Lucia, Dominica, Grenada and Trinidad and Tobago [papa-bois-c01]. He is called the \"keeper of the forest\" and seen as the protector of its flora and fauna [papa-bois-c02], and he is married to Mama Dlo [papa-bois-c03].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "papa-bois-c01",
        "papa-bois-c02",
        "papa-bois-c03"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua pendek berkuku belah dan berjenggot daun yang berlari lebih cepat daripada rusa [papa-bois-c04], dengan tubuh berbulu seperti keledai dan tanduk kecil di dahi [papa-bois-c05]. Ia membawa tanduk banteng berongga untuk memperingatkan hewan saat pemburu datang [papa-bois-c06], dan dapat berubah menjadi rusa untuk menyesatkan pemburu di hutan [papa-bois-c07].",
      "en": "He is pictured as a short old man with cloven hooves and a beard of leaves who runs faster than a deer [papa-bois-c04], with donkey-like hair and small horns on his forehead [papa-bois-c05]. He carries a hollowed-out bull's horn to warn animals of hunters [papa-bois-c06], and can turn into a deer to lead hunters astray in the forest [papa-bois-c07].",

      "id": "papa-bois-c01",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "Papa Bois or Papa Bwa (otherwise known as \"Maître Bois\", meaning master of the woods or \"Daddy Bouchon\" meaning hairy man), a French patois word for \"father wood\" or \"father of the forest\" is a popular fictional folklore character of St. Lucia, Dominica, Grenada and Trinidad and Tobago.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Papa Bois atau Papa Bwa (juga disebut Maître Bois dan Daddy Bouchon), dari kata patois Prancis yang berarti \"bapak hutan\", adalah tokoh cerita rakyat St. Lucia, Dominica, Grenada, serta Trinidad and Tobago.",

      }
    },

      "locator": "Introduction",

      "statement": {

      }

      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "He is married to the Mama Dlo also known as Mama D'Leau or Mama Glo.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menikah dengan Mama Dlo, yang juga dikenal sebagai Mama D'Leau atau Mama Glo.",
        "en": "He is married to Mama Dlo, also known as Mama D'Leau or Mama Glo."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c04",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "His appearance is thought to be that of a short, old man of African descent with cloven hooves (or at least his left leg ends in a large hoof) and a beard of leaves, who, despite his age sports strong muscles and can run faster than a deer.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua pendek keturunan Afrika berkuku belah dan berjenggot daun, berotot kuat, dan dapat berlari lebih cepat daripada rusa.",
        "en": "He is pictured as a short old man of African descent with cloven hooves and a beard of leaves, strongly muscled and able to run faster than a deer."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c05",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "His body is completely covered with hair like that of a donkey and small horns sprout from his forehead.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tubuhnya tertutup bulu seperti keledai dan tanduk kecil tumbuh di dahinya.",
        "en": "His body is covered with donkey-like hair and small horns sprout from his forehead."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c06",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "He is also known to carry a hollowed-out bull's horn, which he uses to warn animals of hunters' approach.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia membawa tanduk banteng berongga untuk memperingatkan hewan saat pemburu mendekat.",
        "en": "He carries a hollowed-out bull's horn to warn animals of approaching hunters."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c07",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "He is also known to have the power of metamorphosis and is commonly thought to transform himself into a deer, luring hunters deep into the forest and getting them lost",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat berubah wujud, biasanya menjadi rusa, untuk memancing pemburu jauh ke dalam hutan hingga tersesat.",
        "en": "He can change shape, commonly into a deer, luring hunters deep into the forest until they are lost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c08",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "It is believed that if one meets Papa Bois, one must be polite and refrain from staring at his hooves, and say a polite greeting to him.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang yang bertemu Papa Bois dipercaya harus bersikap sopan, tidak menatap kukunya, dan memberi salam dengan santun.",
        "en": "Anyone who meets Papa Bois is believed to need to be polite, avoid staring at his hooves, and greet him politely."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "papa-bois-c09",
      "source_id": "papa-bois-s1",
      "quote": "In Nalo Hopkinson's book Midnight Robber, Papa Bois is the name of the large \"daddy trees\" that function as both shelter and ecosystem for sentient creatures.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam buku Midnight Robber karya Nalo Hopkinson, Papa Bois adalah nama pohon-pohon besar yang menjadi tempat berlindung dan ekosistem bagi makhluk berakal.",
        "en": "In Nalo Hopkinson's Midnight Robber, Papa Bois is the name of large trees that serve as shelter and ecosystem for sentient creatures."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Tidak ada gambar Commons yang terbukti menggambarkan makhluk ini; artikel Wikipedia-nya tidak memuat ilustrasi makhluk tersebut."

      "searched": "Hanya artikel Wikipedia yang ditemukan dan bisa dibuka untuk makhluk ini; tidak ada sumber akademik atau lembaga yang bisa dikutip langsung."
    }
  ]
}

    "id": "Pé de garrafa (\"kaki botol\") adalah makhluk legenda cerita rakyat Brasil yang kakinya bulat seperti dasar botol dan meninggalkan jejak bundar [pe-de-garrafa-c01].",

    {
      "id": "Pé de garrafa adalah makhluk legenda Brasil yang dinamai dari kakinya yang bulat seperti dasar botol [pe-de-garrafa-c01]. Ia konon tinggal di hutan dan bertubuh tinggi [pe-de-garrafa-c02]. Seruannya yang melengking membuat pemburu mengira ada teman yang tersesat [pe-de-garrafa-c03], dan mengikutinya justru makin menyesatkan hingga dapat menimbulkan igauan [pe-de-garrafa-c04].",
      "en": "Pé de garrafa is a Brazilian legendary creature named for its rounded, bottle-bottom foot [pe-de-garrafa-c01]. It is said to live in the forest and to be tall [pe-de-garrafa-c02].   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c01",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "Pé de garrafa or pé-de-garrafa (literally \"bottle foot\") is a legendary creature from Brazilian folklore, so-called because it has a rounded foot or feet like the bottom of a bottle, which leaves strange round footprints.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pé de garrafa (\"kaki botol\") adalah makhluk legenda cerita rakyat Brasil yang dinamai demikian karena kakinya bulat seperti dasar botol dan meninggalkan jejak bundar yang aneh.",
        "en": "Pé de garrafa (\"bottle foot\") is a legendary creature of Brazilian folklore, named for its rounded foot like the bottom of a bottle, which leaves strange round footprints."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c02",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "It is said to be forest-dwelling and quite tall.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini konon tinggal di hutan dan bertubuh cukup tinggi.",
        "en": "It is said to live in the forest and to be quite tall."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c03",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "The creature issues shrill calls in the forest, which can confuse hunters into believing it is a lost companion.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini mengeluarkan seruan melengking di hutan yang membuat pemburu mengira itu teman yang tersesat.",
        "en": "The creature makes shrill calls in the forest that make hunters think it is a lost companion."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c04",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "But using the call as a guide will further confuse the pursuer, as the sound bounces off and amplifies and can cause delirium and madness.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mengikuti seruan itu justru makin menyesatkan, karena suaranya memantul, menguat, dan dapat menimbulkan igauan dan kegilaan.",
        "en": "Following the call confuses the pursuer further, as the sound bounces, amplifies and can cause delirium and madness."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c05",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "The creature is blamed for witchcraft. But information is lacking whether it poses more direct threat to humans as a man-eater",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini dituduh melakukan sihir, tetapi tidak ada keterangan apakah ia juga memangsa manusia.",
        "en": "The creature is blamed for witchcraft, but it is unclear whether it is a man-eater."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c06",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "The Pé de garrafa was first described by Alfredo do Vale Cabral (1884), who characterized it as a local legend of the state of Piauí.",
      "locator": "Localization",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pé de garrafa pertama kali dideskripsikan oleh Alfredo do Vale Cabral (1884) sebagai legenda lokal negara bagian Piauí.",
        "en": "The Pé de garrafa was first described by Alfredo do Vale Cabral (1884) as a local legend of the state of Piauí."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c07",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "It is also known as the monster who leaves huge footprints in the sandy and clayey soil of Massapê, Ceará, also in northeastern Brazil.",
      "locator": "Localization",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini juga dikenal sebagai monster yang meninggalkan jejak besar di tanah berpasir dan liat di Massapê, Ceará, di timur laut Brasil.",
        "en": "It is also known as the monster leaving huge footprints in the sandy and clayey soil of Massapê, Ceará, in northeastern Brazil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c08",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "Cascudo wrote that it is a variant of the Mapinguari and Capelobo legends, however, there is no testimony that Pé de garrafa is a maneater like the others.",
      "locator": "Parallels",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cascudo menulis bahwa makhluk ini varian legenda Mapinguari dan Capelobo, meskipun tidak ada kesaksian bahwa ia pemakan manusia seperti keduanya.",
        "en": "Cascudo wrote that it is a variant of the Mapinguari and Capelobo legends, though there is no testimony that it is a man-eater like them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pe-de-garrafa-c09",
      "source_id": "pe-de-garrafa-s1",
      "quote": "Gustavo Barroso wrote it was a \"species of Caapora\" (Caipora), while Vale Cabral (1884) only suggested it shared the same forest habitat as the Caapora.",
      "locator": "Parallels",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gustavo Barroso menyebutnya \"sejenis Caapora\" (Caipora), sedangkan Vale Cabral (1884) hanya menyebut keduanya berbagi habitat hutan yang sama.",
        "en": "Gustavo Barroso called it a \"species of Caapora\" (Caipora), while Vale Cabral (1884) only said it shared the Caapora's forest habitat."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Phantome (di Guyana disebut Moongazer) adalah hantu sangat tinggi dalam cerita rakyat Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago, dan Guyana yang berdiri mengangkangi persimpangan jalan pada malam bulan purnama [phantome-c01, phantome-c02].",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Phantome adalah sosok cerita rakyat Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago, dan Guyana, yang di Guyana disebut Moongazer [phantome-c01]. Ia digambarkan sebagai hantu sangat tinggi yang berdiri di persimpangan jalan pada malam bulan purnama dengan kaki mengangkangi jalan [phantome-c02].",
      "en": "The Phantome is a folklore figure of Saint Lucia, Trinidad and Tobago and Guyana, called the Moongazer in Guyana [phantome-c01]. It is described as an extremely tall spectre standing at crossroads on full-moon nights with its legs straddling the road [phantome-c02].",

        "phantome-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Orang yang lewat di bawahnya dan mengganggu tatapannya ke bulan akan diremukkan [phantome-c03], dengan siulan melengking sebagai satu-satunya peringatan [phantome-c04]. Ia dapat menjadi tak terlihat, hanya bayangannya yang tampak di bawah cahaya bulan [phantome-c05]. Dalam beberapa versi, pejalan yang mengitarinya dengan tenang selamat, sedangkan yang mengejeknya dimakan otaknya [phantome-c06].",
      "en": "Anyone who passes beneath it and interrupts its moongazing is crushed [phantome-c03], with a shrill whistle as the only warning [phantome-c04]. It can become invisible, revealed only by its shadow in the moonlight [phantome-c05].       "statement": {

    {
      "id": "phantome-c02",
      "source_id": "phantome-s1",
      "quote": "It is described as an extremely tall spectre that stands at crossroads on nights of the full moon, its legs wide apart and straddling the road.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

      "id": "phantome-c03",
      "source_id": "phantome-s1",
      "quote": "According to the legend, anyone who tries to pass beneath the figure, interrupting its moongazing, is crushed when it snaps its legs shut.",

      "statement": {

      "id": "phantome-c06",
      "source_id": "phantome-s1",
      "quote": "In some tellings, a traveller who walks around it quietly is left unharmed, but one who taunts it or distracts its gaze from the moon has their brain consumed through the palm of its hand.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Resurrection Mary adalah cerita hantu \"penumpang yang menghilang\" di kawasan Chicago, tentang perempuan muda bergaun putih yang menghilang di Resurrection Cemetery [resurrection-mary-c01, resurrection-mary-c05, resurrection-mary-c06].",
    "en": "Resurrection Mary is a Chicago-area \"vanishing hitchhiker\" ghost story about a young woman in a white dress who vanishes at Resurrection Cemetery [resurrection-mary-c01, resurrection-mary-c05, resurrection-mary-c06].",
    "claim_ids": [
      "resurrection-mary-c01",
      "resurrection-mary-c05",
      "resurrection-mary-c06"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Resurrection Mary adalah cerita hantu jenis \"penumpang yang menghilang\" di kawasan Chicago [resurrection-mary-c01] dan dianggap hantu paling terkenal di kota itu [resurrection-mary-c03]. Hantu ini konon tinggal di Resurrection Cemetery di Justice, Illinois [resurrection-mary-c02].",
      "en": "Resurrection Mary is a Chicago-area \"vanishing hitchhiker\" ghost story [resurrection-mary-c01] and is considered the city's most famous ghost [resurrection-mary-c03]. The ghost is said to reside in Resurrection Cemetery in Justice, Illinois [resurrection-mary-c02].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "resurrection-mary-c01",
        "resurrection-mary-c02",
        "resurrection-mary-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sejak tahun 1930-an, pengemudi di Archer Avenue melaporkan memberi tumpangan kepada perempuan muda [resurrection-mary-c04] bergaun pesta putih, berambut pirang, dan bermata biru [resurrection-mary-c05], yang menghilang saat mereka mendekati pemakaman [resurrection-mary-c06]. Menurut legendanya, Mary tewas ditabrak sepulang berdansa di Oh Henry Ballroom dan dimakamkan dengan gaun dansa putih [resurrection-mary-c07, resurrection-mary-c08, resurrection-mary-c09].",
      "en": "Since the 1930s, drivers on Archer Avenue have reported picking up a young woman [resurrection-mary-c04] in a white party dress with blonde hair and blue eyes [resurrection-mary-c05], who vanishes as they near the cemetery [resurrection-mary-c06]. According to the legend, Mary was killed by a hit-and-run driver after dancing at the Oh Henry Ballroom and buried in a white dancing dress [resurrection-mary-c07, resurrection-mary-c08, resurrection-mary-c09].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "resurrection-mary-c04",
        "resurrection-mary-c05",

        "resurrection-mary-c08",
        "resurrection-mary-c09"
      ]

      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "Resurrection Mary is a well-known Chicago-area ghost story, of the \"vanishing hitchhiker\" type, a type of folklore that is known in many cultures.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Resurrection Mary adalah cerita hantu terkenal di kawasan Chicago, dari jenis \"penumpang yang menghilang\" yang dikenal di banyak budaya.",
        "en": "Resurrection Mary is a well-known Chicago-area ghost story of the \"vanishing hitchhiker\" type, known in many cultures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c02",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "According to the story, the ghost resides in Resurrection Cemetery in Justice, Illinois, a few miles southwest of Chicago.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut ceritanya, hantu ini tinggal di Resurrection Cemetery di Justice, Illinois, beberapa mil di barat daya Chicago.",
        "en": "According to the story, the ghost resides in Resurrection Cemetery in Justice, Illinois, southwest of Chicago."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c03",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "Resurrection Mary is considered to be Chicago's most famous ghost.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Resurrection Mary dianggap hantu paling terkenal di Chicago.",
        "en": "Resurrection Mary is considered Chicago's most famous ghost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c04",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "Since the 1930s, several men driving northeast along Archer Avenue between the Willowbrook Ballroom and Resurrection Cemetery have reported picking up a young female hitchhiker.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak tahun 1930-an, sejumlah pria yang berkendara di Archer Avenue antara Willowbrook Ballroom dan Resurrection Cemetery melaporkan memberi tumpangan kepada seorang perempuan muda.",
        "en": "Since the 1930s, several men driving along Archer Avenue between the Willowbrook Ballroom and Resurrection Cemetery have reported picking up a young female hitchhiker."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c05",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "This young woman is dressed somewhat formally in a white party dress, and is said to have light blonde hair and blue eyes.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Perempuan itu berpakaian pesta putih yang agak formal, berambut pirang terang, dan bermata biru.",
        "en": "The young woman wears a somewhat formal white party dress and has light blonde hair and blue eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c06",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "As the driver nears Resurrection Cemetery, she disappears into it.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika pengemudi mendekati Resurrection Cemetery, ia menghilang ke dalamnya.",
        "en": "As the driver nears Resurrection Cemetery, she disappears into it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c07",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "The story goes that Mary had spent the evening dancing with a boyfriend at the Oh Henry Ballroom.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut ceritanya, Mary menghabiskan malam dengan berdansa bersama kekasihnya di Oh Henry Ballroom.",
        "en": "The story goes that Mary spent the evening dancing with a boyfriend at the Oh Henry Ballroom."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c08",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "She had not gone far when she was struck and killed by a hit-and-run driver, who fled the scene, leaving Mary to die.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Belum jauh berjalan, ia ditabrak hingga tewas oleh pengemudi yang melarikan diri.",
        "en": "Not far along, she was struck and killed by a hit-and-run driver who fled."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c09",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "They buried her in Resurrection Cemetery, wearing a beautiful white dancing dress and matching dancing shoes.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dimakamkan di Resurrection Cemetery dengan gaun dansa putih dan sepatu dansa yang serasi.",
        "en": "She was buried in Resurrection Cemetery in a white dancing dress and matching dancing shoes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c10",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "Jerry Palus, a Chicago southsider, reported that in 1939, he met a person whom he came to believe was Resurrection Mary at the Liberty Grove and Hall at 47th and Mozart",
      "locator": "Reported sightings",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jerry Palus, warga Chicago, melaporkan bahwa pada tahun 1939 ia bertemu seseorang yang ia yakini Resurrection Mary di Liberty Grove and Hall.",
        "en": "Jerry Palus, a Chicago southsider, reported meeting a person he came to believe was Resurrection Mary at the Liberty Grove and Hall in 1939."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c11",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "She also reportedly burned her handprints into the wrought-iron fence around the cemetery in August 1976, although officials at the cemetery have stated that a truck had damaged the fence with no evidence of a ghost.",
      "locator": "Reported sightings",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Agustus 1976 ia konon membakar bekas tangannya pada pagar besi pemakaman, tetapi pengelola pemakaman menyatakan pagar itu rusak ditabrak truk tanpa bukti adanya hantu.",
        "en": "In August 1976 she reportedly burned her handprints into the cemetery fence, though cemetery officials said a truck had damaged it with no evidence of a ghost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "resurrection-mary-c12",
      "source_id": "resurrection-mary-s1",
      "quote": "In 1999, Chicago author Ursula Bielski documented a possible connection to Anna \"Marija\" Norkus, who died in a 1927 auto accident while on her way home from the Oh Henry Ballroom",
      "locator": "Identity of Mary",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada tahun 1999, penulis Chicago Ursula Bielski mencatat kemungkinan kaitannya dengan Anna \"Marija\" Norkus, yang tewas dalam kecelakaan mobil tahun 1927 sepulang dari Oh Henry Ballroom.",
        "en": "In 1999, Chicago author Ursula Bielski documented a possible link to Anna \"Marija\" Norkus, who died in a 1927 car accident on her way home from the Oh Henry Ballroom."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "sharlie-c04",

      "quote": "In August 1944, the serpent was reportedly seen by several groups of people who described it as 30 to 35 feet in length with a dinosaur-like head, pronounced jaws, humps like a camel, and shell-like skin.",
      "locator": "Modern history",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Agustus 1944, beberapa kelompok orang melaporkan melihatnya sepanjang 30 hingga 35 kaki, berkepala mirip dinosaurus, berpunuk seperti unta, dan berkulit seperti cangkang.",
        "en": "In August 1944, several groups reported it as 30 to 35 feet long with a dinosaur-like head, camel-like humps and shell-like skin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sharlie-c05",
      "source_id": "sharlie-s1",
      "quote": "In 1954 A. Boone McCallum, Editor of The Star News held a contest to name the serpent of Payette Lake.",
      "locator": "Modern history",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada tahun 1954, A. Boone McCallum, editor The Star News, mengadakan sayembara untuk menamai ular Payette Lake.",
        "en": "In 1954, A. Boone McCallum, editor of The Star News, held a contest to name the Payette Lake serpent."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sharlie-c06",
      "source_id": "sharlie-s1",
      "quote": "Sharlie was reportedly sighted dozens of times between 1956 and the last documented sighting in 1997.",
      "locator": "Modern history",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sharlie dilaporkan terlihat puluhan kali antara tahun 1956 dan penampakan terakhir yang tercatat pada tahun 1997.",
        "en": "Sharlie was reportedly sighted dozens of times between 1956 and the last documented sighting in 1997."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Sirena Chilota adalah makhluk air setengah ikan setengah manusia dalam mitologi Chilote, putri bungsu Millalobo sang raja laut [sirena-chilota-c01, sirena-chilota-c03, sirena-chilota-c04].",
    "en": "The Sirena Chilota is a half-fish, half-human aquatic being of Chilote mythology, youngest daughter of Millalobo, king of the sea [sirena-chilota-c01, sirena-chilota-c03, sirena-chilota-c04].",
    "claim_ids": [
      "sirena-chilota-c01",
      "sirena-chilota-c03",
      "sirena-chilota-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Sirena Chilota adalah makhluk air dalam mitologi Chilote [sirena-chilota-c01] yang mungkin berasal dari perpaduan Sumpall Mapuche dan putri duyung Eropa [sirena-chilota-c02]. Ia setengah ikan setengah manusia, berambut pirang panjang, dan bersisik emas [sirena-chilota-c03], serta tinggal di dekat Pulau Laitec di Kepulauan Chiloé [sirena-chilota-c07].",
      "en": "The Sirena Chilota is an aquatic being of Chilote mythology [sirena-chilota-c01] that may combine the Mapuche Sumpall and the European mermaid [sirena-chilota-c02]. She is half fish and half human with long blonde hair and golden scales [sirena-chilota-c03], and lives near the island of Laitec in the Chiloé Archipelago [sirena-chilota-c07].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "sirena-chilota-c01",
        "sirena-chilota-c02",
        "sirena-chilota-c03",
        "sirena-chilota-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia putri bungsu Millalobo dan Huenchula [sirena-chilota-c04], bertugas menggembalakan ikan [sirena-chilota-c05], dan membantu Pincoya dan Pincoy membawa pelaut yang tenggelam ke Caleuche [sirena-chilota-c06]. Ia dapat menggiring ikan ke perahu pelaut yang disukainya [sirena-chilota-c08], tetapi lagu cintanya memikat pria [sirena-chilota-c09] yang lalu dibawanya ke istana di dasar laut [sirena-chilota-c10].",
      "en": "She is the youngest daughter of Millalobo and Huenchula [sirena-chilota-c04], tasked with shepherding fish [sirena-chilota-c05], and helps the Pincoya and Pincoy carry drowned sailors to the Caleuche [sirena-chilota-c06]. She may drive fish to the boats of sailors she likes [sirena-chilota-c08], but her love songs lure men [sirena-chilota-c09] whom she takes to a palace at the bottom of the ocean [sirena-chilota-c10].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "sirena-chilota-c04",

        "sirena-chilota-c06",

        "en": "According to Chilote legend, especially among fishermen, she is the youngest daughter of Millalobo, king of the sea, and the human Huenchula."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c05",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "Millalobo gave her the task of shepherding and caring for the fish.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Millalobo memberinya tugas menggembalakan dan merawat ikan.",
        "en": "Millalobo gave her the task of shepherding and caring for the fish."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c06",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "She also helps her siblings (the Pincoya and Pincoy) carry the bodies of drowned sailors to the Caleuche.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia membantu saudara-saudaranya, Pincoya dan Pincoy, membawa jasad pelaut yang tenggelam ke Caleuche.",
        "en": "She helps her siblings, the Pincoya and Pincoy, carry drowned sailors to the Caleuche."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c07",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "The Sirena Chilota is lives near the island of Laitec in the Chiloé Archipelago.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sirena Chilota tinggal di dekat Pulau Laitec di Kepulauan Chiloé.",
        "en": "The Sirena Chilota lives near the island of Laitec in the Chiloé Archipelago."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c08",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "The Sirena Chilota may also help sailors she likes by shepherding fish to their boats.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dapat membantu pelaut yang disukainya dengan menggiring ikan ke perahu mereka.",
        "en": "She may help sailors she likes by shepherding fish to their boats."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c09",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "Sailors must be wary though, as she usually sings alluring love songs.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pelaut harus waspada karena ia biasanya menyanyikan lagu cinta yang memikat.",
        "en": "Sailors must be wary, as she usually sings alluring love songs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c10",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "The Sirena Chilota then brings them to the bottom of the ocean to live in a palace with her family.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sirena Chilota lalu membawa pria yang terpikat ke dasar laut untuk tinggal di istana bersama keluarganya.",
        "en": "She then brings the lured men to the bottom of the ocean to live in a palace with her family."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c11",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "His future descendants are born with fish tails, and the rest of the town will know he slept with the Sirena Chilota.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keturunan pria yang lolos darinya lahir dengan ekor ikan.",
        "en": "The descendants of a man who escapes her are born with fish tails."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sirena-chilota-c12",
      "source_id": "sirena-chilota-s1",
      "quote": "The Sirena Chilota plays an important role in the film Caleuche.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sirena Chilota berperan penting dalam film Caleuche.",
        "en": "The Sirena Chilota plays an important role in the film Caleuche."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "Sisimito adalah kriptid mirip gorila yang berjalan tegak dalam cerita rakyat Belize dan Honduras [sisimito-c01], juga dikenal di Guatemala dan Nicaragua [sisimito-c02]. Ia berjari empat tanpa ibu jari dan berkaki terbalik [sisimito-c03]. Namanya diyakini berasal dari kata Nahuatl Tzitzimitl [sisimito-c05], dan orang Garifuna menyebutnya Sismidu [sisimito-c04].",
      "en": "The Sisimito is an upright, gorilla-like cryptid of Belizean and Honduran folklore [sisimito-c01], also known in Guatemala and Nicaragua [sisimito-c02]. It has four fingers without thumbs and backward-facing feet [sisimito-c03].   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c01",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "In Belizean and Honduran folklore, the Sisimito (alternatively called Sisimite, Sisimita, Súkara, and Itacayo) is a bipedal upright gorilla-like cryptid that possesses a head much like a human, with long hair or fur covering its body.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Belize dan Honduras, Sisimito (juga Sisimite, Sisimita, Súkara, dan Itacayo) adalah kriptid mirip gorila yang berjalan tegak, berkepala mirip manusia, dan bertubuh berbulu panjang.",
        "en": "In Belizean and Honduran folklore, the Sisimito (also Sisimite, Sisimita, Súkara and Itacayo) is an upright, gorilla-like cryptid with a human-like head and long hair covering its body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c02",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "It is also referred to as \"the Mexican Bigfoot\", though it is also known in the neighboring countries of Guatemala and Nicaragua.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga disebut \"Bigfoot Meksiko\" dan dikenal pula di Guatemala dan Nicaragua.",
        "en": "It is also called \"the Mexican Bigfoot\" and is known in Guatemala and Nicaragua as well."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c03",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "It is said to have four fingers on each hand and no thumbs, ape-like facial features, and backward-facing feet with no knees.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon berjari empat tanpa ibu jari, berwajah seperti kera, dan berkaki terbalik tanpa lutut.",
        "en": "It is said to have four fingers and no thumbs, ape-like features, and backward-facing feet with no knees."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c04",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "Its name differs between ethnic groups, for example, the Garifuna call it Sismidu.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berbeda antarsuku; misalnya orang Garifuna menyebutnya Sismidu.",
        "en": "Its name differs between ethnic groups; the Garifuna call it Sismidu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c05",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "The name has no true English translation and it is believed to have come from the Nahuatl word, Tzitzimitl.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya diyakini berasal dari kata Nahuatl Tzitzimitl.",
        "en": "The name is believed to come from the Nahuatl word Tzitzimitl."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c06",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "According to Honduran legends, it is believed that the Sisimite targets and kidnaps human women.",
      "locator": "Honduras",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda Honduras, Sisimite mengincar dan menculik perempuan manusia.",
        "en": "According to Honduran legends, the Sisimite targets and kidnaps human women."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c07",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "Aguilar also explains that the Sisimite feeds on the wild berries from the mountains and that it is not a carnivorous being or a hunter",
      "locator": "Honduras",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Aguilar, Sisimite memakan buah beri liar dari pegunungan dan bukan pemakan daging atau pemburu.",
        "en": "According to Aguilar, the Sisimite eats wild berries from the mountains and is not a carnivore or hunter."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c08",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "In Belizean legend, the Sisimite is said to also feast on humans.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda Belize, Sisimite juga memangsa manusia.",
        "en": "In Belizean legend, the Sisimite also feasts on humans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c09",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "The Sismite is said to live in high forest areas and lives in caves and on rocky land.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sismite konon tinggal di hutan dataran tinggi, di gua, dan di tanah berbatu.",
        "en": "It is said to live in high forest areas, in caves and on rocky land."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c10",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "Though fierce and malevolent towards humans, he is afraid of water and dogs and will avoid them at all costs.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski ganas dan jahat terhadap manusia, ia takut air dan anjing.",
        "en": "Though fierce and malevolent towards humans, he is afraid of water and dogs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c11",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "The Sisimite is believed to often assume human form in order to lure people out of villages with the purpose of killing them.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sisimite dipercaya sering menyamar sebagai manusia untuk memancing orang keluar desa lalu membunuhnya.",
        "en": "The Sisimite is believed to take human form to lure people out of villages and kill them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c12",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "Because of this, he is sometimes confused with Xtabay or La Llorona.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena itu ia kadang disamakan dengan Xtabay atau La Llorona.",
        "en": "Because of this, he is sometimes confused with Xtabay or La Llorona."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c13",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "the Sisimite is also believed to attack men who are killing animals needlessly or destroying the forest.",
      "locator": "Belize",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sisimite juga dipercaya menyerang pria yang membunuh hewan tanpa perlu atau merusak hutan.",
        "en": "The Sisimite is also believed to attack men who kill animals needlessly or destroy the forest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sisimito-c14",
      "source_id": "sisimito-s1",
      "quote": "If one dances in a circle, the Sisimite will try to follow them. However, due to his backward-facing feet he would trip and fall.",
      "locator": "Outsmarting the Sisimite",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jika seseorang menari melingkar, Sisimite akan mengikutinya, tetapi kakinya yang terbalik membuatnya tersandung dan jatuh.",
